--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 10083
entity_slug: flora-bama
google_review_name: places/ChIJAcqjl0mokIgRnm2_K_q2x7U/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2s5dkxXUndPR2RmY0hKQ05rUnJiMmxGTlhsTVdYYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: Excellent tourist attraction for visitors from other countries who want to experience southern life (or people from other regions of the US who are curious)! I'm from GA and felt right at home.

My visit was Feb. 23, 2026 on a Monday afternoon around 3pm. The gift shop is at the entrance and busier than I expected for the day and time.

We made our way toward the back and this place is MUCH larger that it appears from the street. And my, oh my! So many stairs and so many levels! I'm not sure if there is an elevator, but my old knees wished I'd found one.

People were playing bingo in one area. There was lively music in another. The energy was lively but not over stimulating. The views of the beach were really beautiful. I've been to a lot of beaches on the gulf, and the sugar white sand and turquoise water here is truly probably the prettiest I've seen. That may be season dependent though. I don't usually visit the gulf in February.

I didn't eat here so can't speak to the food, but the atmosphere was friendly and welcoming. I'm sure I'll stop in again when I have more time. If you're in the area, you should drop in to at least check it out. No cover charge before 6pm.

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: a062db8b-cda4-49ba-938d-22ef66a3953b
entity_slug: flora-bama
reviewer_name: Diva in the Woods
rating: 5
body: Excellent tourist attraction for visitors from other countries who want to experience southern life (or people from other regions of the US who are curious)! I'm from GA and felt right at home.

My visit was Feb. 23, 2026 on a Monday afternoon around 3pm. The gift shop is at the entrance and busier than I expected for the day and time.

We made our way toward the back and this place is MUCH larger that it appears from the street. And my, oh my! So many stairs and so many levels! I'm not sure if there is an elevator, but my old knees wished I'd found one.

People were playing bingo in one area. There was lively music in another. The energy was lively but not over stimulating. The views of the beach were really beautiful. I've been to a lot of beaches on the gulf, and the sugar white sand and turquoise water here is truly probably the prettiest I've seen. That may be season dependent though. I don't usually visit the gulf in February.

I didn't eat here so can't speak to the food, but the atmosphere was friendly and welcoming. I'm sure I'll stop in again when I have more time. If you're in the area, you should drop in to at least check it out. No cover charge before 6pm.

approved: true
